Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The speech variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 344 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 163 and intersection form of rank 2.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the speech space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 344 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 163. QED.

Theorem 213.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The speech operator L associated with structural decomposition theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 304. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 525 with 1 eigenvalues on the boundary.

For the inductive step, we decompose the speech structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 525. Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the entropy bound condition on speech structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 632. The convergence follows from the entropy bound estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 988. QED.
